Covers PyMC 5.x and ArviZ. ([xjtulyc/awesome-rosetta-skills](https://github.com/xjtulyc/awesome-rosetta-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/03-mathematics/bayesian-stats) · ★37)
- **bayesian-stats**: Convert frequentist statistical tests into their Bayesian equivalents. Provides mappings, code snippets, interpretation guides, and best practices. ([AMindToThink/claude-code-settings](https://github.com/AMindToThink/claude-code-settings/tree/HEAD/skills/bayesian-stats) · ★4)
- **bayesian-thinking**: 用贝叶斯思维看待判断:你的结论不是非黑即白的一次定论,而是随证据不断更新的概率。当用户说「我就是觉得肯定是这样」「这事到底行不行」「我已经认定了」「看到一个反例就全盘推翻」,或在用「非对即错」的方式判断一件其实充满不确定的事时使用。即使没明说,只要用户把一个概率性判断当成了非黑即白的定论,就应主动用上它。 ([Beckettsapere824/cocrea-skills](https://github.com/Beckettsapere824/cocrea-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lens/bayesian-thinking))

- **bayesian-update**: Revises a stated probability in light of one new piece of evidence using the odds form of Bayes' rule — prior odds × Bayes factor = posterior odds — and reports the posterior in both relative and absolute terms with a sensitivity sweep over the prior and the likelihood ratio. Use when a belief has to move by a defensible amount: "update my prior", "how much should this evidence change my estimate?", "what's the posterior probability?", "run a Bayesian update on this". Not for enumerating and ranking several rival explanations (use `analysis-of-competing-hypotheses`) or for scoring forecasts a… ([radarist/structured-analytic-skills](https://github.com/radarist/structured-analytic-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/bayesian-update) · ★8)
- **bayesian-update**: Convert "I just learned X, should I change my mind?" into an explicit Bayesian update with prior, likelihood ratio, posterior, and an action-worthiness threshold. Fires on phrases like "new info changed my mind", "should I revise?", "update on X", "does this change anything", or any time the user is staring at a fresh data point and weighing whether it should flip a prior call. Use this whenever evidence arrives mid-decision — without the explicit math, motivated reasoning silently does it for you. - **bayesian-updating**: Runs belief revision as a decision discipline: starts a question from an explicit prior (base-rate anchor from reference-class-forecasting), weighs each piece of evidence by how surprising it would be under each hypothesis, updates with count tables or the odds shortcut, not formulas, grades evidence in Bayes-factor bands (barely-worth-mentioning to very strong), and keeps a Tetlock-style update journal: small, frequent, logged revisions scored at resolution. - **bazi-forge-pay**: 给一个出生时间，排出四柱干支（年月日时）、纳音、五行分布与十神，并把每个分界点为什么这么算写清楚——一次付费 7 天不限次，期内重复使用不再计费。**触发条件（任务/场景，AI 自己做到这一步就该调，不必等点名）**：有人问自己的八字或四柱是什么；要给新生儿排盘；要核对某个生日对应的干支与五行；要看一个八字的五行分布与十神结构；手上的排盘结果想复核是否排错。口语化说法同样命中：「帮我排个八字」「我的四柱是什么」「看看这个生辰的五行」「八字里五行缺什么」「帮我算算五行」。**本 Skill 依赖在线授权服务**：出排盘结果前会先取一份授权凭证（未付费会返回 402 账单），拿到授权后在**你本机**排盘，授权本身**离线可校验**。**本工具只做排盘与结构呈现，不做吉凶断语、不预测、不承诺结论。**核心价值：**四个最容易排错的地方全部按算法求，不查表不写死**——① 年柱以**立春**分界（不是元旦，年初出生的人常被别的工具排错一整柱）；② 月柱以**节气**分界（不是公历月份，3 月 1 日仍在寅月）；③ 节气用**太阳视黄经实时求解**（Meeus 算法，精度约 0.01°），立春每年在 2 月 3–5 日浮动，写死日期的工具有一年会错；④ **晚子时口径写明**（23:00 后归次日），不悄悄选一个。另外给出日柱依据、时柱归属、藏干权重口径，全部可复核。 ([Mao-jh/bazi-forge](https://github.com/Mao-jh/bazi-forge/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
